Maybe multiplicative update more directly stable:
Given p, compute q, soft weights w (eta). Compute r_i = ∑ p_l w_{i+l} (same as grad_mom/2). Then p_i^{new} ∝ p_i * exp(-lambda * r_i / (mean(r)+eps)) (since r nonnegative). Center? Projected gradient algorithm:

Let's define `_convolve_series(x)`:
```
def _conv_series(a):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    if len(a) <= 0: return np.array([])
    if len(a) <= 1024:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    fsize = next_fast_len(2*len(a)-1)
    F = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(F*F, fsize)[:2*len(a)-1]
    c[c<0]=0
    return c
```

```
def _coord_descent(p, stop, max_sweeps=1000, seed=1, refine=True):
    n=len(p); p=p.copy(); p/=p.sum(); C=conv(p); best=2*n*C.max(); sweeps=1
    rng=...
    while time<stop and sweeps<max_sweeps:
       improved=False; order=rng.permutation(n)
       for i in order:
          if timeout break
          # if v too small? still
          v=p[i]
          B = np.zeros(L); B[i:i+n]=2*p[i:i+n]; B[2*i]=0  # if 2*i<L (always)
          max_x = max(10*v + 10*n_inv, 80*n_inv, 0.05) # maybe
          # also include v
          G=160
          xs=np.linspace(0,max_x,G)
          # To focus around current, maybe append v, 2v
          # evaluate
          w=xs-v
          line = C + w[:,None]*B
          Mline=line.max(axis=1)
          M=np.maximum(Mline, xs*xs)
          Snew=n? S_new = 1 - v + xs (since current sum1)
          # avoid Snew<=0
          scores=2*n*M/(Snew*Snew)
          idx=np.argmin(scores)
          # if xs length not include exactly current? include v via grid maybe not exact; current score from scores where xs=v approximate. For comparison use best.
          if scores[idx] < best - 1e-13:
              # fine grid around xs[idx]
              dw=max_x/G
              low=max(0, xs[idx]-4*dw); high=min(max_x, xs[idx]+4*dw)
              xs2=np.linspace(low, high, 80)
              ... recompute, choose
              if improves:
                 x=...
                 Cnew=C + (x-v)*B; Cnew[2*i]=x*x  # wait C_new special should be x^2, not C + ...? C_2i currently v^2; B_2i=0; C+... gives v^2. Need set Cnew[2*i]=x*x. But what about k=2i if also in other line? Yes override.
                 Snew=1 - v + x
                 p_update = p.copy(); p_update[i]=x; p_new=p_update/Snew; C_new=Cnew/(Snew*Snew)
                 # But for k=2i, Cnew/ Snew^2 okay.
                 p=p_new; C=C_new; best=scores...
                 improved=True
       sweep +=1
       if not improved and stagnation>...: break? maybe continue with perturb?
    return p,best
```

### `_conv(a)`
Direct for n <= 1200? But FFT overhead for small. Use direct for n <= 2000 maybe. Since n likely <=1024. `np.convolve(a,a)` returns float64. Clip small negative. For n large use FFT.
```
def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 1024: # direct maybe ok
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        f=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
        F=rfft(a,f); c=irfft(F*F,f,n=2*n-1)
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0? Could c -= c.min if negative tiny? For nonnegative, use np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
If a contains tiny negative due to optimization? We clip p before. `np.maximum` costs. Use if c has min<0. Fine.

### `_score_prob(p)`

```
q=_conv(p); return 2.0*len(p)*float(np.max(q))
```

### `_as_prob(x)`
```
x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel(); x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0); x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
s=x.sum()
if s<1e-15: x=np.ones(len(x) or 1)/(...)
else x=x/s
return x
```

```
if n <= 800:

   P=rfft(p,f); W=rfft(w,f)

```

```
# gradient of (m+logZ)/eta wrt p is 2*r/eta *? Let's verify: logsumexp q = m+logZ. derivative = ∑ e/ Z ∂q =2∑w r =2r. Divide by eta: 2r/eta.
```

### L-BFGS softmax ref
Implement with cache? Let's write simple.
```
def _lbfgs_smoothing(p0, time_stop, eta=50, scale=2000.0, maxiter=200, bounds=(-15.,15.)):
    p=_as_prob(p0)
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    # ensure zero-free for log
    pf=p + 1e-12
    pf/=pf.sum()
    z0=np.log(pf[1:]/pf[0])
    z0=np.clip(z0,bounds[0],bounds[1])
    fsize? not needed.
    def softmax(z):
        th=np.empty(n); th[0]=0.; th[1:]=z
        m=float(th.max())
        e=np.exp(th-m)
        zsum=e.sum()
        return e/zsum
    def fun(z):
        p=softmax(z)
        val,_,_,_ = _soft(p, eta)
        return scale*val
    def jac(z):
        p=softmax(z)
        val,gp,_,_ = _soft(p, eta)
        Gamma=float(p@gp)
        grad_z = gp - p*Gamma   # length n
        return scale*grad_z[1:]
    def cb(xk): return time.monotonic() > time_stop or (not np.all(np.isfinite(xk)))
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, z0, jac=jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'maxcor':10, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False}, callback=cb)
    except Exception: return p0
    p_new=softmax(np.clip(res.x,...))
    return p_new
```
Potential issue: `fun` and `jac` recompute `_soft` (conv and cross). L-BFGS may call many. We can cache last z in closure to avoid double when called with same. Since L-BFGS typically calls fun then jac for same x at start. We can implement a mutable cache dict:
```
cache={'z':None,'val':None,'grad':None}
def _compute(z):
  key=None; compare np.array_equal? Use `np.allclose` too costly. Use `cache_z is z`? z may be same array? Not guaranteed. Could store last value `last_z_vals`? Simpler no cache.
```
Double cost maybe acceptable for few runs. But can optimize by using a class with `__call__` where `self.f` sets `self.p`? In L-BFGS, it calls `fun(xk)` then `jac(xk)` maybe with same x array object? We can cache based on `z` id? If xk new array each time, id changes. Could store last z and if `np.array_equal(last_z, z)` not too bad length383. Let's do a cache with `last_z`, `last_val`, `last_grad`. In fun: if not cache or not np.array_equal(z, last_z): compute; store. In jac: same. This adds array_equal cost length383 minor. Good.

But line search may call fun many; cache not used for new. Fine.

`_soft` inside uses `_conv` and `_cross`; for n=384 direct cross. Good.

Eta values: we can call sequentially with p improved. For each, time limit. Start eta=20 maybe broad, then 60, 150. Or start high? Smooth low eta might move to minimize average okay. Target max, high useful. We can use eta=20 for global then 80 then 200. scale maybe 1000. We can update p and accept if exact score improves. ### Global parametric details
Let's implement Chebyshev basis.
```
def _cheb_basis(n, d):
    x = 2*np.arange(n)/(n-1)-1 if n>1 else np.zeros(1)
    T0=np.ones(n); T1=x
    cols=[]
    for m in range(1,d+1):
       if m==0: cols.append(T0)
       elif m==1: cols.append(T1)
       else: Tm=2*x*T1 - T0; cols.append(Tm); T0,T1=T1,Tm
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    return B
```
But if start m=1, need recurrence correctly. Simpler:
```
T_pm1=np.ones(n); T_p=x
cols=[]
for m in range(1,d+1):
   if m==1: cols.append(T_p); continue
   T_m=2*x*T_p - T_pm1
   cols.append(T_m)
   T_pm1,T_p=T_p,T_m
```
Could include m=0 constant? To allow overall scale, softmax constant cancels, so no need. But if no constant, z mean not fixed; not necessary. However missing constant can allow shape scale but not all shifts? Constant irrelevant. Good.
For symmetric-only, use m even (2,4,...). For asym full, all m.

```
def _resample(p,L):
    if len(p)==L: return p.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(p)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,L)
    return _as_prob(np.interp(x_new,x_old,p))
```

### Detailed `_coord_descent` code
Let's write robust:
```
def _coord_descent(p, stop, seed=1, min_sweeps=1, max_stall=3, verbose=False):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    C=_conv(p); best=2*n*float(C.max())
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ninv=1.0/n
    if n <= 400: G = 160
    elif n <= 800: G = 100
    else: G = 70
    no_improve=0
    mult=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < stop and no_improve < max_stall:
        improved=False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        # maybe random + cyclic? okay
        for step_i in range(n):
            i=int(order[step_i])
            if time.monotonic() > stop: break
            v=float(p[i])
            # Build B
            B = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            # p slice length n from i to i+n? But p length n; start=i, end=min(i+n,n)? Actually k-i from 0 to n-1 => k from i to i+n-1. B[k]=2p[k-i] where k-i in [0,n-1]. So B[i:i+n] = 2*p[0:n], not p[i:i+n]! Wait earlier I made mistake! Let's re-evaluate: For coordinate i, C_k = ∑_j p_j p_{k-j}. Changing p_i affects terms where j=i: p_i p_{k-i} => index k-i. So B_k = 2 p_{k-i}, with k-i from 0..n-1. Thus for k from i to i+n-1, B_k =2 p_{k-i}, where k-i ranges 0..n-1 => B[i:i+n]=2*p[0:n]? Actually if k=i + l, B=2 p_l. So B[i:i+n] = 2*p[0:n]? Let's test n=4, i=1: B_k for k=1: 2p0, k=2:2p1, k=3:2p2, k=4:2p3. So slice length4 should be 2*p[0:4], not p[1:5]. Earlier B[i:i+n]=2*p[i:i+n] was wrong. Need correct!
```

```
def _parametric_optimize(n, degree, p_init, time_stop, seed=0, eta=40, scale=1000, maxiter=400):
    B = _poly_basis(n, degree)
    if B.size==0: return p_init
    # z0 from log p_init
    logp = np.log(p_init + 1e-12)
    try: c0 = np.linalg.lstsq(B, logp, rcond=None)[0]
    except: c0 = np.zeros(B.shape[1])
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -10, 10)
    c0 = c0 + rng.normal(0,0.2,c0.size)
    c0=np.clip(c0,-10,10)
    def softmax_z(c): z=B@c; p=softmax(z)
    cache...
    def fun(c): val=... ; return scale*val
    def jac(c): gp; Gamma; grad_z=gp-p*Gamma; return scale*(B.T @ grad_z)
    minimize...
```

### `_lbfgs_soft` details cache
```
def _lbfgs_soft(p_init, stop, eta=50, scale=1000, maxiter=120, ref=None):
    p_init=_as_prob(p_init); n=len(p)
    if ref is None: ref=int(np.argmax(p_init))
    indices=[i for i in range(n) if i!=ref]
    pf=clip(p_init,1e-15,1); z0=np.log(pf[indices]/pf[ref]); z0=clip(z0,-15,15)
    cache = {}
    def _calc(z):
      if 'z' in cache and np.array_equal(cache['z'], z): return cache['val'],cache['gradz'],cache['p']
      theta=zeros(n); theta[ref]=0; theta[indices]=z
      zmax=theta.max(); e=np.exp(theta-zmax); p=e/e.sum()
      val,gp,_,_ = _soft(p,eta)
      Gamma=float(np.dot(p,gp))
      grad_full=gp - p*Gamma
      gradz=grad_full[indices]
      cache['z']=z.copy(); cache['val']=val; cache['gradz']=gradz; cache['p']=p
      return val,gradz,p
    def fun(z):
      if not finite z: return 1e100
      val,_,_=_calc(z); return scale*val
    def jac(z):
      if not finite: return zeros_like(z)
      _, gradz,_=_calc(z); return scale*gradz
    def cb(xk): return time.monotonic() > stop or (not np.all(np.isfinite(xk)))
    try: res=minimize(...)
    except: return p_init
    p_new=_calc(res.x if res.x is not None else z0)[2]
    return p_new
```
Need `indices` array for fast: `idx=np.array([i for i...])`. Then `theta[idx]=z`, `gradz=grad_full[idx]`.

`_soft` returns gp = 2*r/eta. F=m+logZ / eta. If eta small, gp magnitude 2/eta*r large? If eta=20, 0.1*r ~0.0001. scale 1000 ->0.1. Good.

Maybe use `eta` values based on n? qmax~0.002, gaps small. For exact max, need η qgap large; qgap relative maybe 1e-4, η=200 gives 20. Use 100,300. But high eta weights few active, gradient noise. Use sequence [20,80,250]. Parametric maybe 50.

### LP A construction with correct coefficient
Constraint p*g ≤ M. c_k(p,g)=∑_j p_j g_{k-j}. Coefficient for g_j is p_{k-j}. For row k, j valid from max(0,k-n+1) to min(k,n-1). Coefficient p[k-j]. If loop j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j] = p? For row k in [j, j+n-1], k-j in [0,n-1]; A[k,j] should p[k-j]. Loop j sets A[j + l, j] = p[l]. So `A[j:j+n, j] = p`. Correct.
If p sum1, M=qmax. Feasible g=p. Good.

HiGHS `b_ub` maybe all M. But LP maximizing sum g; if M very small and p values positive, A rows small; scale okay. If M=0 (all p zero impossible). Use M+1e-15.

### Final code flow in detail
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    final_reserve = min(30.0, max(5.0, budget*0.05))
    hard_stop = t0 + budget - final_reserve
    rng = default_rng(seed)

    parent = _get_parent()
    p = _as_prob(parent)
    if p.size==0: p=np.ones(384)/384
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=_score_prob(p)
    # ensure exact eval? maybe not now.

    def refresh(cand):
       nonlocal best_p,best_sc
       if cand is None: return
       c=_as_prob(cand)
       s=_score_prob(c)
       if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_p=c.copy(); best_sc=s

    # If budget tiny: validate and return
    if hard_stop < t0 + 5: finalize...

    # 1. Coord initial for up to 120s or until no improve
    if time < hard_stop-100:
       p1,s1=_coord_descent(p, min(hard_stop-200, t0+120), seed=seed)
       refresh(p1)
       if s1 < best_sc: p=p1
```

Coordinate code implement inner function `_eval_xs(xs, v, B, C, n)`:
```
w = xs - v
# Ensure shapes
line = C + w[:,None] * B
M = line.max(axis=1)
M = np.maximum(M, xs*xs)
Snew = 1.0 - v + xs
scores = np.full_like(xs, np.inf)
valid = Snew > 1e-12
scores[valid] = (2.0*n*M[valid])/(Snew[valid]*Snew[valid])
idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
return idx, scores
```
For `C + w[:,None]*B`, if G large, memory. Use `np.multiply(w[:,None], B, out=w[:,None])`? simpler.
If B all zero? p zero impossible. If C maybe contains negative? clip.

Fine: choose low/high. Use `best_x = xs[idx]`. `dw = (xs[-1]-xs[0])/(len(xs)-1)`.
`lo=max(0, best_x-5*dw); hi=min(xmax, best_x+5*dw)`. If hi-lo<1e-12, use [best_x]. Evaluate xs2. Need current score best maybe at v not grid. If best_x from coarse but fine not improve, still update if coarse score < best.
```
chosen_x, chosen_sc = grid_best
if chosen_sc < best - 1e-13:
   # fine may improve
   # after fine if chosen_sc < best...
```

#### `_as_prob`
```
def _as_prob(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.ones(384)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(384)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a
```
If x is list of bool? clip okay; bool to float.

#### `_conv`
```
def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        fs = next_fast_len(2*n - 1)
        F = rfft(a, fs)
        c = irfft(F*F, fs, n=2*n-1)
    # clip negative tiny
    if c.size:
        mn = float(c.min())
        if mn < 0:
            if mn > -1e-10:
                c = c.copy()
                c[c<0]=0.0
            else:
                c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
`np.convolve` returns new. For FFT, `irfft` returns length n if specify; okay. `c = np.ascontiguousarray(c)`.

#### `_cross`
```
def _cross(p,w,n):
    if n == 0: return empty
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(p[::-1], w, mode='full')
        return c[n-1:2*n-1]
    fs=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    P=rfft(p,fs); W=rfft(w,fs)
    c=irfft(P*W,fs,n=3*n-2)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
p,w nonnegative. Direct with p[::-1] negative stride may be slower; maybe `p_rev = p[::-1]` but view. Could use `np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])`. For n<=512, contiguous reverse cost small. Use `p[::-1]` maybe np.convolve handles.

#### `_score_prob`

```
def _score_prob(p):
    p=_as_prob(p)
    q=_conv(p)
    if q.size==0: return math.inf
    m=float(q.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m): return math.inf
    return 2.0*len(p)*m
```
This normalizes every time, could be overhead. In hot loops we ensure p normalized; use `_score_prob_fast` no norm:
```
def _score_norm(p):
    q=_conv(p)
    return 2.0*len(p)*float(q.max())
```
But to be safe in final, use `_score_prob`. In loops, use `_score_norm` and know sum1. We'll define both maybe.

```
def _score_norm(p):
    assert p is normalized? no assert.
    q=_conv(p); m=q.max(); return 2*len(p)*float(m)
```
If p has tiny negative? we maintain.

#### `_soft`
If p not normalized? For L-BFGS p normalized. We'll use normalized.
```
def _soft(p, eta):
    q = _conv(p)
    if q.size == 0: return inf...
    m = float(q.max())
    ex = np.exp(eta * (q - m))
    Z = float(ex.sum())
    if Z <= 0 or not finite: w = np.zeros_like(q); w[0]=1
    else: w = ex / Z
    r = _cross(p, w, len(p))
    val = (m + math.log(max(Z, 1e-300))) / eta
    grad = (2.0 / eta) * r
    return val, grad, q, w
```
`math.log` if Z very small. q max at least? fine.

#### `_lbfgs_soft`
Need guard if n<2 (z length0): return p.
```
def _lbfgs_soft(p_init, stop, eta=50.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=120):
    p0=_as_prob(p_init)
    n=len(p0)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p0
    ref=int(np.argmax(p0))
    idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != ref], dtype=int)
    pf=np.clip(p0, 1e-15, None)
    z0=np.log(pf[idx] / pf[ref])
    z0=np.clip(z0, -15.0, 15.0)
    cache_z = [None]; cache_data=[None]
    def calc(z):
       if cache_z[0] is not None and np.array_equal(cache_z[0], z):
          return cache_data[0]
       theta=np.zeros(n)
       theta[ref]=0.0
       theta[idx]=z
       zm=float(theta.max())
       e=np.exp(theta-zm)
       s=float(e.sum())
       if s <=0 or not finite: p = p0.copy()
       else: p=e/s
       val,gp,_,_ = _soft(p, eta)
       if not finite val: p=p0.copy(); val=0; gp=zeros
       else:
           Gamma=float(np.dot(p,gp))
           grad_full = gp - p*Gamma
       data=(val, grad_full[idx], p)
       cache_z[0]=z.copy(); cache_data[0]=data
       return data
```
But if line search calls with nonfinite, calc may error. In fun return large if not finite z.
`grad_full[idx]` returns copy? okay.
```
    def fun(z):
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100
       val,_,_ = calc(z); return scale*val
    def jac(z):
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return np.zeros_like(z)
       _, gz,_ = calc(z); return scale*gz
    def cb(xk):
       return (time.monotonic() > stop) or (not np.all(np.isfinite(xk)))
    try:
       res = minimize(fun, z0, jac=jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxcor':10, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False}, callback=cb)
    except Exception:
       return p0
    # if res.x finite
    try:
       _, _, pnew = calc(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).clip(-15,15))
       return pnew
    except: return p0
```
Potential issue: `calc` stores cache_z as z reference; next call may mutate z? L-BFGS passes new arrays; no. `np.array_equal` with large arrays okay.
If `res.success` false but x finite, pnew still maybe improved; accept externally.

#### `_poly_basis`
```
def _poly_basis(n, degree):
    if n <=1 or degree <=0: return np.empty((n,0))
    x = (np.arange(n) - (n-1)/2.0) / max(1.0,(n-1)/2.0)  # [-1,1]
    # Chebyshev T_m
    T0 = np.ones(n)
    T1 = x.copy()
    cols=[]
    if degree >=1: cols.append(T1)
    for m in range(2, degree+1):
       Tm = 2*x*T1 - T0
       cols.append(Tm)
       T0, T1 = T1, Tm
    B = np.column_stack(cols)
    # Normalize columns to unit norm to stabilize c bounds
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(B[:,j])
       if nrm>0: B[:,j]/=nrm
    return B
```
Note x formula: `(np.arange(n)-0.5)/n`? To center 0: (i - (n-1)/2)/((n-1)/2) gives endpoints -1,1. For n=1 avoid. Use if n>1.

#### `_parametric_optimize`
```
def _parametric_optimize(n, degree, p_init, stop, seed=0, eta=40.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=500):
    p0=_as_prob(p_init)
    B=_poly_basis(n, degree)
    if B.size==0 or time.monotonic()>=stop:
       return p0
    d=B.shape[1]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # init from p0
    logp=np.log(p0 + 1e-12)
    try: c0=np.linalg.lstsq(B, logp, rcond=None)[0]
    except: c0=np.zeros(d)
    c0=np.nan_to_num(c0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c0=np.clip(c0, -10, 10)
    c0 = c0 + rng.normal(0, 0.3, d)
    c0=np.clip(c0, -10,10)
    bounds=[(-10.0,10.0)]*d
    cache...
    def calc(c):
       if cache ... array_equal: return
       z=B @ c
       zm=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-zm); s=e.sum(); p=e/s
       val,gp,_,_=_soft(p,eta)
       Gamma=np.dot(p,gp); gradz=gp-p*Gamma; gradc=B.T@gradz
       return val,gradc,p
    def fun(c): ... scale*val
    def jac(c): ... scale*gradc
    cb time
    try res=minimize(fun,c0,jac=jac,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={...},callback=cb)
    except: return p0
    if res.x finite: _,_,pnew=calc(res.x); return pnew
    else return p0
```
Could include using `maxiter=800`; d small. `ftol=1e-10, gtol=1e-5`.
Maybe `scale=2000`.

Potential issue: Chebyshev basis normalized; c bounds 10 => z range maybe large; okay.

### `_coord_descent` code (correct)
Let's write with time checks and recompute C each sweep.

```
def _coord_descent(p, stop, seed=1):
    p = _as_prob(p.copy())
    n = len(p); L=2*n-1
    if n==0 or time.monotonic() > stop: return p, math.inf
    C = _conv(p); best = 2.0*n*float(C.max() if C.size else 0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(best): best=math.inf
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ninv = 1.0/n
    if n <= 300: G=180
    elif n <= 512: G=120
    elif n <= 800: G=80
    else: G=50
    GF=80
    max_stall=2 # after this many no-improve sweeps break
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop:
        # ensure normalized
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12 or not finite: break
        p = p/s
        C = _conv(p)
        current = 2.0*n*float(C.max())
        if math.isfinite(current) and current < best: best=current
        improved=False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for ii in range(n):
            i=int(order[ii])
            if time.monotonic() > stop: break
            v=float(p[i])
            B = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            start=i
            end=i+n
            if end > L: end=L
            # start <= L always i; length L-start = min(n,2n-1-i)=n? for i<=n-1, end=i+n <=2n-1? If i=n-1, end=2n-1=L, length n. Good.
            B[start:end] = 2.0 * p[:end-start]  # end-start = n always? If end clipped? For i=n-1, end=L=2n-1, end-start=n. yes.
            special = 2*i
            if 0 <= special < L:
                B[special] = 0.0
            # max_x
            max_x = max(10.0*v, 80.0*ninv, 0.15)
            max_x = min(1.0, max_x)
            xs = np.linspace(0.0, max_x, G)
            # ensure include current v? Could add if not too close.
            # For evaluation function:
            def eval_grid(arr):
                w = arr - v
                # If w zero? no
                line = C + w[:,None] * B
                M = line.max(axis=1)
                M = np.maximum(M, arr*arr)
                Snew = 1.0 - v + arr
                sc = np.full(arr.shape, math.inf)
                good = Snew > 1e-12
                sc[good] = (2.0*n*M[good])/(Snew[good]*Snew[good])
                idx = int(np.argmin(sc))
                return idx, float(sc[idx]), arr[idx]
            # optionally add v to grid?
            if not np.any(np.abs(xs - v) < 1e-3):
                xs_extra = np.concatenate([xs, [v]])
                # unique? Not necessary, but evaluation includes v; okay. But xs sorted not needed.
            else: xs_extra=xs
            idx, scv, xbest = eval_grid(xs_extra)
            # fine grid
            if scv < best - 1e-13:
                # Determine dw; if xs_extra not sorted use range
                dw = max_x / max(1, G-1)
                lo = max(0.0, xbest - 5*dw)
                hi = min(max_x, xbest + 5*dw)
                if hi - lo > 1e-12:
                    xsf = np.linspace(lo, hi, GF)
                    # include xbest and v?
                    xsf = np.unique(np.concatenate([xsf, [xbest, v]]))
                    idxf, scf, xf = eval_grid(xsf)
                    if scf < scv:
                        scv, xbest = scf, xf
            if scv < best - 1e-13:
                x = float(xbest)
                Cnew = C + (x - v) * B
                if 0 <= special < L:
                    Cnew[special] = x*x
                Cnew = np.maximum(Cnew, 0.0)
                Snew = 1.0 - v + x
                if Snew > 1e-12:
                    pnew = p.copy()
                    pnew[i] = x
                    pnew /= Snew
                    C = Cnew / (Snew*Snew)
                    p = pnew
                    best = scv
                    improved = True
        if improved:
            stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= max_stall:
                break
    # final recompute
    p = _as_prob(p)
    C = _conv(p)
    sc = 2.0*n*float(C.max())
    return p, sc
```
Potential variable `special` inside evaluation closure? It uses `B`, `C`, `v`; fine. But inside eval_grid, `arr*arr` for xs. Good.
`xs_extra` not sorted; `dw` uses max_x/G; fine. `np.unique` sorts.
Could use `math.inf` inside numpy `np.full` needs float `np.inf` not math.inf? It accepts `math.inf`. Fine.
Potential performance: Defining eval_grid per coordinate overhead; acceptable. Could inline for clarity. But okay.

However, `line = C + w[:,None] * B` returns shape (G,L). If L huge and G small. For `xs_extra` with extra v length G+1, okay.
Memory Jika n=768 G80 L1535 -> 98k elements; two arrays ~1.5MB. Good.

`B[start:end] = 2.0 * p[:end-start]` where end-start = n. For n large, copies p. Good. But if p array changed? no.

If p has extremely small sum? normalized.

At the end, `best` may be lower than exact due to C drift; final recompute returns actual.

No-improve halt after 2 sweeps. But a sweep with tiny improvements <1e-13 considered no; maybe too strict. Use 1e-12? Good. If stuck, main perturbs.

Could add "cyclic order" instead random to ensure all coords. Random permutation okay.

### `_pair_polish`
Implement with full conv. Need p normalized. Use `_score_norm` on unnormalized? We can compute directly as above.
```
def _pair_polish(p, stop, seed=1, max_sweeps=20, trials=40):
    p=_as_prob(p); n=len(p); best=2*n*float(_conv(p).max())
    rng=default_rng(seed); ninv=1/n; stall=0
    while time<stop and stall<2:
        improved=False
        for _ in range(trials):
           if time>stop: break
           i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i==j: continue
           oi=float(p[i]); oj=float(p[j])
           vals_i = _pair_vals(oi, ninv)
           vals_j = _pair_vals(oj, ninv)
           best_pair=(oi,oj); best_s=best
           for x in vals_i:
             for y in vals_j:
               Snew=1.0-oi-ox+x+y? need o_j variable name `oj`
               if Snew <= 1e-10: continue
               p[i]=x; p[j]=y
               qmax=float(_conv(p).max())
               sc=2*n*qmax/(Snew*Snew)
               if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-13:
                   best_s=sc; best_pair=(x,y)
           p[i]=oi; p[j]=oj
           if math.isfinite(best_s) and best_s < best - 1e-13:
               Snew=1 - oi - oj + best_pair[0] + best_pair[1]
               p[i],p[j]=best_pair
               if Snew>1e-12: p /= Snew
               best=best_s; improved=True
        if improved: stall=0
        else: stall+=1
    p=_as_prob(p); sc=_score_norm(p); return p,sc
```
Need rest sum can be 0 if n=2 and we zero both; skip if Snew<0.01? For normalized sum, Snew may be 0 but invalid; skip.
`_pair_vals` as:
```
def _pair_vals(v, ninv):
    vals = np.array([0.0, v, 0.5*v, 2.0*v, 5.0*v, 0.3*ninv, 1.0*ninv, 3.0*ninv], dtype=float)
    vals = vals[vals >= 0.0]
    vals = np.unique(vals)
    vals = vals[vals <= 1.0]
    if vals.size == 0: vals=np.array([0.0])
    return vals
```
Up to 8 values; 64 conv. Good. If current v=0.5, 5v=2.5 clipped>1 -> excluded. Maybe exclude huge; but coordinate can handle. Pair only local.

### `_perturb`
```
def _perturb(p, sigma, seed):
    p=_as_prob(p); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if sigma <= 0: return p
    # multiplicative lognormal
    noise = rng.standard_normal(p.size)
    p2 = p * np.exp(0.2 * sigma * noise)
    # optionally occasional large coordinate changes? maybe add random spikes
    if rng.random() < 0.3:
        k=int(rng.integers(p.size)); p2[k] += 0.05
    p2=np.clip(p2,0,None); s=p2.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return p
    return p2/s
```
Sigma values 0.5,1,2. The 0.2 factor: sigma=2 => exp(0.4*noise) moderate. Could use 0.5. We'll use `0.25*sigma`.

### `_lp_step`
```
def _lp_step(p, time_limit=5.0):
    p=_as_prob(p); n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    if n > 900: return None
    q=_conv(p); M=float(q.max())
    if M <=0: return None
    A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = p
    c=-np.ones(n)
    bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L, M), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
       return None
    x=res.x
    if x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
    x=np.clip(x,0,None); s=x.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return None
    # feasibility
    if float(np.max(A.dot(x))) > M*(1+1e-6)+1e-10:
       # maybe scale down? infeasible; return None
       # try clip? no
       pass
    x/=s
    return x
```
A.dot for n=384 cheap. If infeasible due presolve tolerance, maybe still usable? We can allow if max violation <1e-5*M. Else scaling: `scale = M / max(Ax)` if max>0; x=x*scale; rescale. This could restore feasibility but reduce sum. Let's do:
```
Ax=A.dot(x); viol=Ax.max()
if viol > M and viol>0:
    x *= M/viol
    s=x.sum(); if s>... x/=s; return x (constraint feasible but sum1)
```
But scaling may change sum, okay. If max(Ax)<M, can scale up to M if some Ax>0? Since homogeneous, scale x by M/viol to saturate; then normalize. We can always scale to max=M (unless M=0) and normalize. But if direction x has max(Ax)<M, scaling up until max=M keeps feasible? If all Ax <= M, scaling by M/max(Ax) makes max=M, yes feasible. Do that to get extreme. But if max(Ax)=0, return None.
```
mAx=np.max(A@x)
if mAx > 0:
    x *= M/mAx
x/=x.sum()
```
After scaling, all constraints ≤M because originally ≤M (or if infeasible scaled down). If original infeasible with mAx>M, scaling down makes all ≤M. Good. Then feasible. Thus LP direction usable even if infeasible (just scaled). Nice.
But if original infeasible badly, normalized x may be feasible. Return.

### `_resample`
```
def _resample(p, L):
    p=_as_prob(p)
    if p.size == L: return p.copy()
    old=np.linspace(0,1,p.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,L)
    q=np.interp(new, old, p)
    return _as_prob(q)
```

### Time in `_coord_descent` if stop is in past returns immediately. It recomputes C each sweep; if stop only a few seconds, one sweep.

### Ensure no `eval_grid` closure retains huge arrays after coordinate? Python GC.

### Potential optimization in `_coord_descent`: `line.max(axis=1)` computes max over 2nd axis; if G=1 and shape (1,L), okay. `M=np.maximum(M, arr*arr)` returns new.

### Potential numerical issue: C + wB with w negative can make line negative; max may be from special x^2. But actual coefficients nonnegative; if line negative due to floating or formula? For w=-v, C_new should nonnegative. We take max over line which may include negative values but max with C positive. If due formula, line at some k could be -eps; M negative? But C nonnegative and B nonnegative, if w=-v, for k with B large, C_k should be at least vB_k/v? Actually C_k may be less than vB_k? Since B_k=2p_{k-i}; C_k includes v*2p_{k-i} plus other positive if changed coordinate terms? At w=-v (x=0), C_k - v B_k = C_k - v*2p_{k-i}. This removes cross term but if C_k also includes v^2 at k=2i and self? Should be nonnegative. Numerical errors can make tiny negative; M maybe negative if all line negative? but special x^2=0, M>=0. KKT condition for min M with p variable: At optimum, active constraints q_k=M, λ_k≥0. Gradient of ∑λ_k q_k minus β sum zero. We can alternate:

```
for iter:
  p normalized
  T p = cross? T p_i = ∑ p_j λ_{i+j}
  p_new = p / (Tp / mean(Tp))? or p * (mean/Tp)
  normalize
```
This is multiplicative with r_i = Tp_i (same as cross correlation p,λ). It seeks T p constant. It doesn't explicitly reduce M but follows KKT. #### `_get_parent`
```
def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if h is None:
            h = ()
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.sum() <= 0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size if arr.size else 384)
    return arr
```

#### `_score_norm`
```
def _score_norm(p):
    q=_conv(np.asarray(p,float))
    if q.size==0: return math.inf
    m=float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0: return math.inf
    return 2.0 * len(p) * m
```

```
        sym = 0.5 * (c + c[::-1])
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is None:
            h = ()
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr


def _as_prob(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        fs = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        F = rfft(a, fs)
        c = irfft(F * F, fs, n=2 * n - 1)
    if c.size and float(c.min()) < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _cross(p, w, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 512:
        pr = np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
        c = np.convolve(pr, w, mode="full")
        return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    fs = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    P = rfft(p, fs)
    W = rfft(w, fs)
    c = irfft(P * W, fs, n=3 * n - 2)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _score_norm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    q = _conv(p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * float(len(p)) * m


def _soft(p, eta):
    n = int(len(p))
    q = _conv(p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return math.inf, np.zeros(n), np.empty(0), np.empty(0)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    e = np.exp(eta * (q - m))
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(Z):
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        w[0] = 1.0
        val = m / eta if eta > 0 else m
    else:
        w = e / Z
        val = (m + math.log(Z)) / eta
    r = _cross(p, w, n)
    grad = (2.0 / eta) * r
    return val, grad, q, w


def _lbfgs_soft(p_init, stop, eta=50.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=120):
    p0 = _as_prob(p_init)
    n = int(p0.size)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p0
    ref = int(np.argmax(p0))
    idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != ref], dtype=int)
    pf = np.clip(p0, 1e-15, None)
    z0 = np.log(pf[idx] / pf[ref])
    z0 = np.clip(z0, -15.0, 15.0)

    cache_z = [None]
    cache_d = [None]

    def calc(z):
        if cache_z[0] is not None and np.array_equal(cache_z[0], z):
            return cache_d[0]
        theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        theta[ref] = 0.0
        theta[idx] = z
        zm = float(theta.max())
        e = np.exp(theta - zm)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            p = p0.copy()
            val = 1e100
            gz = np.zeros_like(z)
        else:
            p = e / s
            val, gp, _, _ = _soft(p, eta)
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                p = p0.copy()
                val = 1e100
                gz = np.zeros_like(z)
            else:
                Gamma = float(np.dot(p, gp))
                grad_full = gp - p * Gamma
                gz = grad_full[idx]
        data = (val, gz, p)
        cache_z[0] = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        cache_d[0] = data
        return data

    def fun(z):
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            return 1e100
        val, _, _ = calc(z)
        return scale * val

    def jac(z):
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            return np.zeros_like(z)
        _, gz, _ = calc(z)
        return scale * gz

    def cb(xk):
        return (time.monotonic() > stop) or (not np.all(np.isfinite(xk)))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun, z0, jac=jac, method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-6,
                     "maxcor": 10, "maxls": 20, "disp": False},
            callback=cb,
        )
    except Exception:
        return p0
    try:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)
        val, _, pnew = calc(x)
        return pnew if math.isfinite(val) or val < 1e95 else p0
    except Exception:
        return p0


def _poly_basis(n, degree):
    n = int(n)
    degree = int(degree)
    if n <= 1 or degree <= 0:
        return np.empty((n, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - (n - 1) / 2.0) / max(1.0, (n - 1) / 2.0)
    T0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    T1 = x.copy()
    cols = [T1]
    for m in range(2, degree + 1):
        Tm = 2.0 * x * T1 - T0
        cols.append(Tm)
        T0, T1 = T1, Tm
    B = np.column_stack(cols)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
        nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(B[:, j]))
        if nrm > 0.0:
            B[:, j] /= nrm
    return B


def _parametric_optimize(n, degree, p_init, stop, seed=0, eta=40.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=500):
    p0 = _as_prob(p_init)
    n = int(n)
    B = _poly_basis(n, degree)
    if B.size == 0 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    logp = np.log(p0 + 1e-12)
    try:
        c0 = np.linalg.lstsq(B, logp, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        c0 = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    c0 = np.where(np.isfinite(c0), c0, 0.0)
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -10.0, 10.0)
    c0 = c0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, B.shape[1])
    c0 = np.clip(c0, -10.0, 10.0)

    bounds = [(-10.0, 10.0)] * B.shape[1]
    cache_c = [None]
    cache_d = [None]

    def calc(c):
        if cache_c[0] is not None and np.array_equal(cache_c[0], c):
            return cache_d[0]
        z = B @ c
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            return (1e100, np.zeros_like(c), p0.copy())
        zm = float(z.max())
        e = np.exp(z - zm)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = p0.copy()
            val = 1e100
            gc = np.zeros_like(c)
        else:
            p = e / s
            val, gp, _, _ = _soft(p, eta)
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                p = p0.copy()
                val = 1e100
                gc = np.zeros_like(c)
            else:
                Gamma = float(np.dot(p, gp))
                grad_z = gp - p * Gamma
                gc = B.T @ grad_z
                gc = np.where(np.isfinite(gc), gc, 0.0)
        data = (val, gc, p)
        cache_c[0] = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        cache_d[0] = data
        return data

    def fun(c):
        val, _, _ = calc(c)
        return scale * val

    def jac(c):
        _, gc, _ = calc(c)
        return scale * gc

    def cb(xk):
        return (time.monotonic() > stop) or (not np.all(np.isfinite(xk)))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun, c0, jac=jac, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds,
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-11, "gtol": 1e-5,
                     "maxcor": 12, "maxls": 24, "disp": False},
            callback=cb,
        )
    except Exception:
        return p0
    try:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.clip(x, -10.0, 10.0)
        val, _, pnew = calc(x)
        return pnew if math.isfinite(val) or val < 1e95 else p0
    except Exception:
        return p0


def _coord_descent(p, stop, seed=1):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() > stop:
        return p, _score_norm(p)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    C = _conv(p)
    best = 2.0 * n * float(C.max()) if C.size else math.inf
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ninv = 1.0 / float(n)

    if n <= 300:
        G = 180
    elif n <= 512:
        G = 120
    elif n <= 800:
        G = 80
    else:
        G = 50
    GF = 80
    NEG = -1.0e100
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop:
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(s):
            break
        p = p / s
        C = _conv(p)
        cur = 2.0 * n * float(C.max()) if C.size else math.inf
        if math.isfinite(cur) and cur < best:
            best = cur

        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for ii in range(n):
            i = int(order[ii])
            if time.monotonic() > stop:
                break
            v = float(p[i])
            B = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            st = i
            en = i + n
            if en > L:
                en = L
            if en > st:
                B[st:en] = 2.0 * p[:en - st]
            special = 2 * i
            if 0 <= special < L:
                B[special] = 0.0

            max_x = max(10.0 * v, 80.0 * ninv, 0.15)
            max_x = min(1.0, float(max_x))
            xs = np.linspace(0.0, max_x, G, dtype=np.float64)
            if not np.any(np.abs(xs - v) <= 1e-5):
                xs = np.unique(np.concatenate([xs, [v]]))

            def eval_grid(arr):
                arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
                if arr.size == 0:
                    return 0, math.inf, 0.0
                w = arr - v
                line = C + w[:, None] * B
                if 0 <= special < L:
                    line[:, special] = NEG
                M = line.max(axis=1)
                M = np.maximum(M, arr * arr)
                Snew = 1.0 - v + arr
                sc = np.full(arr.shape, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
                good = Snew > 1e-12
                sc[good] = (2.0 * n * M[good]) / (Snew[good] * Snew[good])
                ix = int(np.argmin(sc))
                return ix, float(sc[ix]), float(arr[ix])

            _, scv, xbest = eval_grid(xs)
            if math.isfinite(scv) and scv < best - 1e-13:
                dw = max_x / max(1, G - 1)
                lo = max(0.0, xbest - 5.0 * dw)
                hi = min(max_x, xbest + 5.0 * dw)
                if hi - lo > 1e-12:
                    xsf = np.linspace(lo, hi, GF, dtype=np.float64)
                    xsf = np.unique(np.concatenate([xsf, [xbest, v]]))
                    _, scf, xf = eval_grid(xsf)
                    if math.isfinite(scf) and scf < scv:
                        scv, xbest = scf, xf

            if math.isfinite(scv) and scv < best - 1e-13:
                x = float(xbest)
                Cnew = C + (x - v) * B
                if 0 <= special < L:
                    Cnew[special] = x * x
                Cnew = np.maximum(Cnew, 0.0)
                Snew = 1.0 - v + x
                if Snew > 1e-12:
                    pnew = p.copy()
                    pnew[i] = x
                    pnew /= Snew
                    C = Cnew / (Snew * Snew)
                    p = pnew
                    best = scv
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                break

    p = _as_prob(p)
    sc = _score_norm(p)
    return p, sc


def _kkt_flatten(p, stop, seed=0, max_iters=150):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p, _score_norm(p)
    best_sc = _score_norm(p)
    stall = 0
    alphas = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop:
            break
        q = _conv(p)
        if q.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(q.max())
        temp = 0.25 / float(n)
        w = np.exp((q - M) / max(temp, 1e-15))
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
            w = np.zeros_like(q)
            w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / ws
        r = _cross(p, w, n)
        rm = float(r.mean())
        if rm <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(rm):
            break
        z = (r - rm) / rm
        z = np.clip(z, -5.0, 5.0)
        best_local = (p.copy(), best_sc)
        for a in alphas:
            p2 = p * np.exp(-a * z)
            s2 = float(p2.sum())
            if s2 <= 1e-12:
                continue
            p2 = p2 / s2
            sc2 = _score_norm(p2)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_local[1] - 1e-13:
                best_local = (p2, sc2)
        if best_local[1] < best_sc - 1e-13:
            p, best_sc = best_local
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 3:
                break
    return p, best_sc


def _perturb(p, sigma, seed=1):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if sigma <= 0.0:
        return p
    noise = rng.standard_normal(p.size)
    p2 = p * np.exp(0.25 * float(sigma) * noise)
    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        k = int(rng.integers(p.size))
        p2[k] += 0.04 + 0.05 * float(sigma)
    p2 = np.clip(p2, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return p
    return p2 / s


def _lp_step(p, time_limit=5.0):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n > 900 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    q = _conv(p)
    M = float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(M):
        return None
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = p
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(L, M, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(time_limit), "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    x = res.x
    if x is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    Ax = A.dot(x)
    mAx = float(Ax.max())
    if mAx > 0.0:
        x *= M / mAx
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return x / s


def _resample(p, L):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 1:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)
    if p.size == L:
        return p.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    q = np.interp(new, old, p)
    return _as_prob(q)


def _pair_polish(p, stop, seed=1):
    p = _as_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= stop:
        return p, _score_norm(p)
    best = _score_norm(p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ninv = 1.0 / float(n)

    def vals(v):
        arr = np.array([0.0, v, 0.5 * v, 2.0 * v, 5.0 * v,
                        0.3 * ninv, 1.0 * ninv, 3.0 * ninv], dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr[arr >= 0.0]
        arr = arr[arr <= 1.0]
        arr = np.unique(arr)
        if arr.size == 0:
            arr = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
        return arr

    stall = 0
    for sweep in range(12):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop:
            break
        improved = False
        for _ in range(35):
            if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                break
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            oi, oj = float(p[i]), float(p[j])
            vi = vals(oi)
            vj = vals(oj)
            local_best = best
            local_pair = (oi, oj)
            for x in vi:
                for y in vj:
                    Snew = 1.0 - oi - oj + float(x) + float(y)
                    if Snew <= 1e-10:
                        continue
                    p[i] = float(x)
                    p[j] = float(y)
                    try:
                        qmax = float(np.max(_conv(p)))
                    except Exception:
                        p[i], p[j] = oi, oj
                        break
                    sc = 2.0 * n * qmax / (Snew * Snew)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best - 1e-13:
                        local_best = sc
                        local_pair = (float(x), float(y))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            p[i], p[j] = oi, oj
            if math.isfinite(local_best) and local_best < best - 1e-13:
                x, y = local_pair
                Snew = 1.0 - oi - oj + x + y
                if Snew > 1e-10:
                    p[i] = x
                    p[j] = y
                    p /= Snew
                    best = local_best
                    improved = True
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 2:
                break
    p = _as_prob(p)
    return p, _score_norm(p)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    seed = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42

    if budget > 300.0:
        final_reserve = min(320.0, max(60.0, 0.25 * budget))
    elif budget > 100.0:
        final_reserve = min(80.0, max(20.0, 0.25 * budget))
    else:
        final_reserve = min(10.0, max(2.0, 0.30 * budget))

    abs_end = t0 + max(2.0, budget - 5.0)
    work_stop = t0 + max(1.0, budget - final_reserve - 5.0)
    if work_stop >= abs_end - 1.0:
        work_stop = abs_end - 1.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    best_p = _as_prob(parent)
    best_sc = _score_norm(best_p)

    def refresh(cand):
        nonlocal best_p, best_sc
        if cand is None:
            return
        c = _as_prob(cand)
        if c.size == 0:
            return
        sc = _score_norm(c)
        sym = 0.5 * (c + c[::-1])
        sym = _as_prob(sym)
        scs = _score_norm(sym)
        if math.isfinite(scs) and scs < sc - 1e-13:
            c, sc = sym, scs
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_p = c.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    if time.monotonic() >= abs_end - 5.0:
        return [float(x) for x in best_p]

    init_time = min(90.0, max(5.0, 0.12 * budget))
    init_stop = min(work_stop, t0 + init_time)
    if init_stop > time.monotonic() + 1.0:
        try:
            pp, _ = _coord_descent(best_p, init_stop, seed=seed + 1)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    for deg in (8, 14, 20):
        if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 50.0:
            break
        opt_stop = min(work_stop - 50.0, time.monotonic() + 12.0)
        try:
            cand = _parametric_optimize(
                best_p.size, deg, best_p, opt_stop,
                seed=seed + 100 + deg, eta=40.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=500
            )
        except Exception:
            cand = best_p
        if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 25.0:
            break
        pol_stop = min(work_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
        try:
            pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 1000 + deg)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_p.size <= 600:
        for eta in (20.0, 60.0, 150.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 85.0:
                break
            opt_stop = min(work_stop - 85.0, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            try:
                cand = _lbfgs_soft(best_p, opt_stop, eta=eta, scale=1000.0, maxiter=120)
            except Exception:
                cand = best_p
            if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 35.0:
                break
            pol_stop = min(work_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 40.0)
            try:
                pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 2000 + int(eta))
                refresh(pp)
            except Exception:
                pass

    for a in range(3):
        if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 65.0:
            break
        lp_tl = min(6.0, max(1.0, max(0.0, work_stop - time.monotonic() - 55.0)))
        if lp_tl <= 0.0:
            break
        try:
            g = _lp_step(best_p, time_limit=lp_tl)
        except Exception:
            g = None
        if g is None:
            continue
        cand = best_p.copy()
        cand_sc = best_sc
        for t in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
            ct = _as_prob((1.0 - t) * best_p + t * g)
            st = _score_norm(ct)
            if math.isfinite(st) and st < cand_sc - 1e-13:
                cand, cand_sc = ct, st
        refresh(cand)
        if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 30.0:
            continue
        pol_stop = min(work_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 30.0)
        try:
            pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 3000 + a)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.monotonic() < work_stop - 30.0:
        kkt_stop = min(work_stop - 15.0, time.monotonic() + 30.0)
        try:
            pp, _ = _kkt_flatten(best_p, kkt_stop, seed=seed + 11000, max_iters=150)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if time.monotonic() < work_stop - 10.0:
            pol_stop = min(work_stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 18.0)
            try:
                pp, _ = _coord_descent(best_p, pol_stop, seed=seed + 12000)
                refresh(pp)
            except Exception:
                pass

    for sig in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        if time.monotonic() >= work_stop - 45.0:
            break
        try:
            cand = _perturb(best_p, sigma=sig, seed=seed + 4000 + int(sig * 10))
        except Exception:
            continue
        pol_stop = min(work_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 30.0)
        try:
            pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 5000 + int(sig * 10))
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_sc > 1.503 and time.monotonic() < work_stop - 120.0:
        n0 = best_p.size
        L = int(min(768, max(128, 2 * n0 if n0 <= 384 else int(round(1.5 * n0)))))
        if L != n0:
            cand = _resample(best_p, L)
            opt_stop = min(work_stop - 100.0, time.monotonic() + 12.0)
            try:
                cand = _parametric_optimize(
                    L, 14, cand, opt_stop,
                    seed=seed + 30000, eta=40.0, scale=1000.0, maxiter=400
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            pol_stop = min(work_stop - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
            try:
                pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 31000)
                refresh(pp)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < work_stop - 15.0:
        pair_stop = min(work_stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 25.0)
        try:
            pp, _ = _pair_polish(best_p, pair_stop, seed=seed + 40000)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_stop = abs_end - 10.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_stop - 2.0:
        try:
            pp, _ = _coord_descent(best_p, final_stop, seed=seed + 50000)
            refresh(pp)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if time.monotonic() < final_stop - 25.0:
            for sig in (0.2, 0.4):
                if time.monotonic() >= final_stop - 15.0:
                    break
                try:
                    cand = _perturb(best_p, sigma=sig, seed=seed + 60000 + int(sig * 10))
                    pol_stop = min(final_stop - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 15.0)
                    pp, _ = _coord_descent(cand, pol_stop, seed=seed + 61000 + int(sig * 10))
                    refresh(pp)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    p_out = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p_out = np.where(np.isfinite(p_out), p_out, 0.0)
    p_out = np.clip(p_out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s_out = float(p_out.sum())
    if p_out.size == 0 or s_out < 0.01:
        p_out = _as_prob(_get_parent())
    else:
        p_out = p_out / s_out

    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p_out]))
    except Exception:
        exact = math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(exact):
        fb = _as_prob(_get_parent())
        try:
            exact_fb = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fb]))
        except Exception:
            exact_fb = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(exact_fb):
            p_out = fb
        else:
            p_out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in p_out]
```
